# Recipe roles

OSRS Wiki contributors, CC BY-NC-SA 3.0. Literal recipe inputs/tools are linked to unambiguous Wiki identities and checked against the copied game cache. Dynamic template expressions, ambiguous names, noncurrent pages and unavailable identities are retained for review. These are organizational uses, not instructions to consume an item, activity recommendations, or proof of skill/quest requirements. An item can be an ingredient for one process and useful equipment for another. No recipe role creates an upgrade ranking.
